We introduce $\textbf{CrEST}$, a hierarchical credit assignment framework that retains RL's verifier-bounded ceiling while incorporating dense token-level signals from a privileged self-teacher. $\textbf{CrEST}$ resolves credit at two levels: turn-segmented verified advantages address inter-turn dilution, while entropy-gated self-teacher modulation refines intra-turn token contributions. Experiments on BFCL V3 and WildToolBench show that $\textbf{CrEST}$ consistently outperforms both RL and distillation baselines across two model scales, with the largest gains on long-trajectory and strict session-level metrics. Our work demonstrates that the teacher's role in policy optimization can be reduced from determining update directions to modulating update magnitudes, unlocking dense credit assignment without sacrificing the verifier-bounded ceiling.

</details>


### [138] [TsuGO: Probing Search Efficiency in LLM Reasoning via Go Life-and-Death Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13221)
*Shunwen Bai,Ziping Ma,Chaoyang Zhang,Yarong Wang,Jiale Liu,Zhen Qin,Qingpei Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TsuGO基准，利用围棋死活题来评估LLM推理中的"搜索效率"，通过将CoT解析为结构化搜索树，分离领域知识与搜索组织能力，揭示当前LLM在搜索组织维度上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理评估从最终答案正确性转向过程级评估，但现有方法只关注CoT的连贯性和冗余度，忽视了模型如何规划推理路径和分配推理资源（即搜索组织）。大多数基准任务可通过静态能力求解，缺少需要候选生成、响应检查、分支比较和回溯的对抗性结构，导致搜索组织这一关键维度未被测量。

Method: TsuGO利用围棋死活题构建过程级推理基准。围棋死活题具有封闭可验证的解空间和内在对抗性结构，使候选生成、分支比较和回溯成为推理的必要组成而非偶然痕迹。通过约束解空间来剥离领域知识与搜索组织，将CoT解析为结构化搜索树，报告搜索效率、Token效率及其他诊断指标和可视化。

Result: 实验表明：当前LLM远未达到稳定的死活题求解水平；更强的模型通过更早找到正确候选并在有效分支上持续投入而成功，但大多数模型行为更接近无引导的搜索算法，而非神经引导的KataGo；更长的CoT或更高的Token效率并不必然意味着更好的搜索。

Conclusion: 研究识别出搜索组织和推理资源分配是LLM推理评估中缺失的关键维度，为过程级推理评估提供了新的方法论基准。

Abstract: The evaluation of LLM reasoning is moving from final-answer accuracy to process-level assessment, yet existing methods still fail to capture how models plan reasoning paths and allocate reasoning resources--that is, how they organize search. Prior process-level methods focus on the coherence and redundancy of chain-of-thought (CoT), and most benchmark tasks have a single objective solvable by static capabilities such as derivation and tool use, leaving search organization unmeasured. We introduce TsuGO, a process-level reasoning benchmark for evaluating Search Efficiency in LLM reasoning through Go life-and-death problems. These problems provide closed and verifiable solution spaces with an inherent adversarial structure, making candidate generation, response checking, branch comparison, and backtracking necessary parts of reasoning rather than incidental trace patterns. By constraining the solution space, TsuGO disentangles domain knowledge from search organization, parses CoT into a structured search tree, and reports Search Efficiency together with Token Efficiency and other diagnostic metrics and visualizations. Experiments show that current LLMs remain far from stable tsumego solving: stronger models succeed by finding the correct candidate earlier and sustaining effort on productive branches, but most models still behave much closer to unguided search algorithms than to neural-guided KataGo. Longer CoT or higher Token Efficiency does not necessarily imply better search. Our results identify search organization and reasoning-resource allocation as missing dimensions in LLM reasoning evaluation.

</details>


### [139] [Capability Sheaves for Compositional Agent-Harness Repair: Controlled Quotients and a Real-Repository Stress Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13228)
*Saveliy Batruin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用有限能力层（capability sheaf）建模多Agent系统中组件间状态不一致的问题，将可接受运行定义为全局截面，并用CSP精确判定、线性化相对上同调类作为诊断特征。受控实验证明其对手性（invariance）机制有效，但在SWE-bench Multilingual真实基准测试中，上同调方法未能展现出超越传统非上同调选择器的实际优势，发现门限测试失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多Agent系统组合了检索、路由、状态管理、溯源和验证等模块，但各组件在局部最优的同时可能对共享状态产生不一致的认知，导致系统失败。作者希望通过代数拓扑（层论与上同调）的工具来系统化建模这种失败模式，并提供可诊断、可搜索的解决方案。

Method: 将组件能力建模为有限能力层：stalk编码类型化行为签名，限制映射保留共享字段，可接受运行对应全局截面。接受性由精确CSP定义；诊断与搜索通过线性化相对上同调类实现。实验分两部分：（1）受控实验引入隐藏中间中介者，通过对消去其coboundary来减少候选预算；（2）在SWE-bench Multilingual/PatchFuseBench上测试，使用候选索引修补和留一仓库排除的弃权门控。

Result: 受控实验中，商化coboundary将候选预算从2000降至1000，对齐隐藏状态后差距消失，验证了不变性机制。真实基准上：池级构造为常数（因[b-Dx]=[b] in coker D）无法排序；候选级修补在848/875个候选上非平凡，在120/160个issue内部变化。该修补解决118个issue，略优于非上同调选择器的116个，但差异在仓库层面不显著（p=0.75）；留一仓库弃权门控达到127/160，与强锚持平。发现门限失败，确认验证集保持密封。

Conclusion: 研究支持了受控不变性机制和可辨识性修正的有效性，但在真实世界的代码修复基准上未能证明上同调方法相对传统方法的优势。作者诚实呈现了失败结果，强调精确CSP与商化方法等价，说明其贡献在于对陈旧代表元的不变性而非推理能力的超越。

Abstract: Agent harnesses combine retrieval, routing, state, provenance, and verification, but locally successful components may disagree on shared state. We model this failure with a finite \emph{capability sheaf}: stalks encode typed behavior signatures, restriction maps retain shared fields, and accepted runs are useful global sections. An exact finite constraint-satisfaction problem (CSP) defines acceptance, while a linearized relative cohomology class provides a diagnostic and search feature.
  A controlled experiment over 20 task clusters introduces hidden interior mediators whose raw states are nuisance variables. Quotienting their coboundaries reduces the candidate budget from 2,000 to 1,000 per cluster; aligning the hidden state removes the gap. Exact CSP matches the quotient, so the result demonstrates invariance to stale representatives, not superiority over exact reasoning.
  We then test the method on a discovery split from the SWE-bench Multilingual pool of PatchFuseBench: 160 issues from 20 repositories, 875 real candidate patches, 2,579 source-aware edit atoms, and 153 newly executed patches. A first pool-level construction is constant because $[b-Dx]=[b]$ in $\operatorname{coker}D$ and therefore cannot rank configurations. A candidate-indexed repair is nontrivial on 848/875 candidates and varies within 120/160 issues. It resolves 118 issues versus 116 for a matched noncohomological selector, but the difference is not supported across repositories (exact sign-flip $p=0.75$). A leave-one-repository-out abstention gate reaches 127/160, tying the strong anchor and exceeding its matched gate by one issue ($p=1.0$). The discovery gate therefore fails and the confirmatory split remains sealed. The study supports the controlled invariance mechanism and an identifiability correction, but not a real-world cohomological advantage.

</details>


### [140] [vToken: Token-Level Virtualization for Reclaimable KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13263)
*Yuanhang Gao,Xiangrui Yang,Yuanfeng Chen,Hongjia Chen,Qianru Lv,Wenfei Wu,Dongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: vToken提出了一种轻量级的token级虚拟化层，通过token-table间接引用和异步重打包机制，解耦逻辑token存活性与物理块放置，解决了PagedAttention块级管理与KV淘汰算法token级粒度不匹配导致的块内碎片问题。在vLLM中实现后，相比Naive-Evict基线减少了27.2%-72.3%的保留KV块，SLA约束吞吐量提升最高1.37倍，并发能力扩展最高2倍，集成代码量从500+行降至50行以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理中KV cache随序列长度和batch size增长成为关键内存瓶颈。PagedAttention虽然用固定大小内存块降低分配器碎片，但KV淘汰算法在token粒度上操作，与块级管理不匹配，导致块内碎片化，使大量已分配KV内存无法回收利用。

Method: vToken引入token级虚拟化层：通过token-table间接引用维护稳定的逻辑token视图，将逻辑token存活性与物理块放置解耦；采用异步重打包机制将活跃token重新紧凑放置，实现物理内存回收。设计保持PagedAttention kernel和CUDA Graph兼容性，在vLLM中实现。

Result: 在H2O、Random和Scissorhands三种KV淘汰策略及多个模型上评估：相比Naive-Evict基线，保留KV块减少27.2%-72.3%，SLA约束吞吐量提升最高1.37倍；在受限active-KV预算下最大可行并发度扩展2倍；每种策略的集成代码量从500+行减少到50行以内。

Conclusion: vToken通过token级虚拟化有效消除了块内碎片问题，显著提升了KV内存利用率和系统吞吐，同时大幅降低了不同淘汰策略的集成成本，验证了将逻辑管理层从物理块管理解耦的实际价值。

Abstract: Large language model serving faces a critical memory bottleneck: the KV cache grows with sequence length and batch size. PagedAttention uses fixed-size memory blocks to reduce allocator-level fragmentation, but recent KV eviction algorithms operate at a token granularity finer than block-level management. This mismatch causes intra-block fragmentation, leaving a large fraction of allocated KV memory unreclaimable. We present vToken, a lightweight token-level virtualization layer that decouples logical token liveness from physical block placement. vToken maintains a stable logical token view through token-table indirection and realizes physical reclamation by repacking live tokens asynchronously. The design preserves PagedAttention kernels and CUDA Graph compatibility. We implement vToken in vLLM and evaluate it with H2O, Random, and Scissorhands across models. Compared with a paired Naive-Evict baseline, vToken reduces retained KV blocks per request by 27.2\%--72.3\% and improves SLA-constrained throughput by up to 1.37$\times$. Under a constrained active-KV budget, it extends the maximum feasible concurrency by up to 2$\times$, while reducing the per-policy integration footprint from 500+ lines to under 50.

</details>


### [141] [Towards Context-Aware Clinical Motion Understanding in Daily Living at Home: Freezing of Gait Detection with Egocentric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13283)
*Vayalet Stefanova,Diwas Lamsal,Margot Genbrugge,Maxim Yudayev,Christian Schlenstedt,Moran Gilat,Bart Vanrumste,Benjamin Filtjens*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探索利用自我中心视觉（egocentric vision）提供的任务情境信息来辅助理解日常生活中的运动，具体通过帕金森病患者的冻结步态（FOG）检测进行验证。在13名患者家中收集的同步自我中心视频、可穿戴IMU和专家标注数据上，比较了预训练自我中心视频基础模型、IMU基础模型以及从头训练的TCN的性能。结果显示IMU-TCN表现最佳（F1=42.3，AUROC=83.0），视频特征虽然不及IMU但也展现出优于随机水平的判别能力，且自我中心视觉可能捕捉到IMU独立于的FOG相关信息，支持将预训练自我中心视频表征作为可穿戴传感器运动理解的情境补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常生活中的运动理解需要超越运动学的背景信息，因为相似的惯性模式可能反映有意停止、物体交互或病理性运动障碍等不同情况。自我中心视觉提供任务相关情境可能有助于消除这些歧义，作者通过帕金森病冻结步态检测这一受情境因素强烈影响的症状来研究该挑战。

Method: 收集13名帕金森病患者在家中的同步自我中心视频、可穿戴IMU数据和专家标注的FOG标签。评估三种方法：预训练自我中心视频基础模型（V-JEPA2）和IMU时序基础模型的冻结表征，以及从头训练的IMU-TCN。采用留一受试者（leave-one-subject-out）交叉验证评估事件检测性能。

Result: IMU-TCN获得最强事件检测性能（F1=42.3，AUROC=83.0），优于V-JEPA2自我中心视频特征（F1=32.6，AUROC=77.2）。Ego-video虽未超越IMU感知，但表现出高于随机的判别能力，且定性分析提示自我中心视觉可能捕捉到与IMU独立且与FOG相关的信息。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持使用预训练自我中心视频表征为基于可穿戴传感器的临床运动理解增添情境信息，自我中心视觉可作为IMU感知的有价值补充而非替代。

Abstract: Understanding motion in daily living requires context beyond kinematics, because similar inertial patterns during activities of daily living (ADLs) can reflect intentional stopping, object interaction, or pathological movement impairment. Egocentric vision provides task-related context that may help disambiguate these cases. We investigate this challenge through freezing of gait (FOG) detection in Parkinson's disease (PD), a symptom strongly influenced by contextual factors during ADLs. Using synchronized egocentric video, wearable IMUs, and expert-annotated FOG labels collected from 13 PD participants in their homes, we evaluate frozen representations from pretrained ego-video and time-series foundation models, alongside an IMU-based TCN trained from scratch, under leave-one-subject-out evaluation. The IMU-based TCN achieved the strongest event-detection performance, reaching 42.3 F1 and 83.0 AUROC, compared with 32.6 F1 and 77.2 AUROC for V-JEPA2 ego-video features. Although ego-video alone did not outperform IMU-based sensing, it showed above-chance discrimination, and qualitative analyses suggest that egocentric vision may capture FOG-relevant information independent of IMUs. Together, these results support the use of pretrained ego-video representations to add contextual information to wearable-sensor-based clinical motion understanding in daily living.

</details>


### [142] [NAS-Driven Hardware Accelerator Exploration for Edge AI and Quantization Effects on the Pareto Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13293)
*Eleftherios Mylonas,Angelos Kouprizas,Michael Birbas,Alexios Birbas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三阶段流水线（硬件无关的Pareto排序代理前端、带Pareto感知过滤与反馈控制的量化桥接、基于CGRA4ML的进化式域空间搜索后端），并实证研究INT4 PTQ对NAS-Bench-201 Pareto空间的影响，发现FP32零样本代理在Pareto空间覆盖上优于专用INT4训练的代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有硬件感知NAS将量化直接纳入搜索循环，导致搜索复杂度增加且架构与量化设计紧密耦合；而更简单的搜索后量化（PTQ）策略缺乏系统分析——PTQ对NAS发现的Pareto结构的影响尚不清楚，且缺乏将量化架构映射到可重构加速器并自动进行硬件探索的框架。

Method: 提出三阶段流水线：(1) 在NAS-Bench-201上构建硬件无关的Pareto排序代理作为前端；(2) 量化桥接层，包含Pareto感知过滤和反馈控制；(3) 在CGRA4ML上使用进化式域空间探索（DSE）后端搜索最优硬件映射。同时通过形式化稳定性指标对全部15,625个架构的真值数据开展实证研究。

Result: 实证研究表明INT4 PTQ会扰动NAS-Bench-201的Pareto空间，并且FP32零样本代理在两种标准搜索策略下的Pareto空间覆盖率均优于专用INT4训练的代理。

Conclusion: 搜索后量化配合硬件无关的Pareto代理可以有效解耦架构搜索与量化设计，降低搜索复杂度，同时保持较好的Pareto空间覆盖性能，为Edge AI部署提供了一条更简单且有效的路径。

Abstract: Edge AI deployment demands neural architectures that are simultaneously accurate, computationally efficient, and hardware-deployable - a challenge addressed by hardware-aware Neural Architecture Search (NAS). While recent works incorporate quantization directly into the NAS loop, these approaches expand search complexity and tightly couple architecture and quantization design. The simpler post-search quantization strategy has received little analytical attention: the effects of Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) on the NAS-discovered Pareto structure remain uncharacterised, and no framework combines quantized architecture mapping onto reconfigurable accelerators with automated hardware exploration. This paper addresses both gaps. First, a three-stage pipeline is proposed: a hardware-agnostic Pareto rank surrogate frontend on NAS-Bench-201, a quantization bridge with Pareto-aware filtering and feedback control, and an evolutionary Domain Space Exploration (DSE) backend on CGRA4ML for optimal hardware mapping. Second, an empirical study characterises how INT4 PTQ perturbs the NAS-Bench-201 Pareto space through formal stability metrics on ground-truth data for all 15,625 architectures, and demonstrates that an FP32 zero-shot surrogate outperforms a dedicated INT4-trained surrogate in Pareto space coverage across two standard search strategies.

</details>


### [143] [StateBridge: Training-free Hidden-state Alignment for Latent Communication in LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13317)
*Yanwen Peng,Delvin Ce Zhang,Xi Wang,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StateBridge是一种无需训练的潜在通信方法，通过封闭形式的正交变换将发送者的最终层隐藏状态对齐到接收者的输入空间，并以连续前缀形式注入接收者，在22/26个模型-任务组合中取得了最佳或并列最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多智能体系统通常使用文本（离散token）通信，但文本是离散瓶颈，将连续的隐藏状态转换为离散token会丢失信息。已有潜在通信方法要么需要逐层注入工作记忆，要么需要训练投影器限制可移植性，因此需要一种训练-free且可移植的潜在通信方案。

Method: StateBridge采用训练-free的潜在通信方式：通过封闭形式的正交变换将发送者的最终层隐藏状态对齐到接收者的输入空间，并辅以轻量级的范数校准和词汇锚定来确保与预训练输入分布的兼容性。对齐后的状态作为连续前缀预置到接收者智能体的输入中。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成和问答三个任务上，使用来自两个模型家族的四种模型进行评估。StateBridge在26个模型-任务组合中的22个上取得最佳或并列最佳分数，一致性地优于最强基线。

Conclusion: StateBridge证明了无需训练的潜在通信方法可以同时克服文本通信的信息瓶颈和已有潜在方法对训练/投影器的依赖，通过在输入空间进行正交对齐实现高效的跨智能体信息传递。

Abstract: Large language model based multi-agent systems usually communicate in text, i.e., using discrete tokens. However, text introduces a discrete bottleneck. Converting the sender's continuous hidden states into discrete tokens discards information that token identities alone cannot capture. Recent work proposes latent communication as an alternative, where agents transmit hidden representations directly without converting them to text. However, existing latent methods either inject working memory layer by layer across the transformers, or require trained projectors that limit portability. We propose StateBridge, a training-free latent communication approach that aligns the sender's final-layer hidden states to the receiver's input space via a closed-form orthogonal transformation. Lightweight norm calibration and vocabulary anchoring ensure compatibility with the pretrained input distribution. The aligned states are prepended to the input of the receiver agent as a continuous prefix. We evaluate StateBridge on math reasoning, code generation, and question answering with four models from two families. StateBridge achieves the best or tied-best score on 22 out of 26 model-task pairs, consistently outperforming the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [144] [LLM-Guided Graph Generation for Structure-Based Local Improvement Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13333)
*Hai Xia,Vaidyanathan Peruvemba Ramaswamy,Stefan Szeider*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向MiniZinc格式约束优化问题的问题无关（problem-agnostic）自动化管道：通过提示大型语言模型（LLM）生成图生成器，将任意问题实例映射为统一加权图，其中节点代表决策变量、边代表约束关系。该图用于结构化的局部改进框架（SLIM）进行变量选择，并从图中提取统一特征用于算法配置选择。在20个MiniZinc竞赛问题的实例上，算法选择对单次Gurobi基线的平均问题加权胜率达到39.5%，超过最佳单一配置（19.3%）的两倍，配置与特征消融后进一步提升至44.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大邻域搜索（LNS）通常随机选择决策变量子集进行迭代优化，但不同领域的问题需要针对性地设计变量选择策略。研究者希望避免依赖人工设计领域特定的变量选择启发式，而是构建一个能够自动适配所有问题的机制，实现自动化结构提取与特征生成。

Method: 1) 利用LLM配合语义指南，为每个MiniZinc格式的问题类型生成图生成器；2) 图生成器将任意实例映射为统一加权图（节点为决策变量，边为约束关系）；3) 该加权图为所有问题实例提供共享的通用图表示，从中提取相同图特征用于配置选择；4) 基于图的特征指导结构化的局部改进框架（SLIM）进行变量选择。

Result: 在20个MiniZinc竞赛问题上，算法选择相对单次Gurobi基线实现39.5%的平均问题加权胜率，是最佳单一配置（19.3%）的两倍以上。通过配置和特征消融，性能进一步提升至44.0%。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的语义生成能够实现有效的自动化结构提取和特征提取，从而为约束优化提供问题无关的通用图表示和算法选择框架，显著优于手工设计的最佳单一配置。

Abstract: Large neighborhood search normally selects a random subset of decision variables for iterative optimization. For efficiently solving different problems, researchers tend to design variable selection strategies by taking into account structural features from different domains. In this paper, we build an automatic pipeline that is problem-agnostic to all problems in the MiniZinc format. By prompting an LLM with our semantic guidelines, we guide the LLM to produce a graph generator that maps any instance of a problem type to a uniform weighted graph, where nodes represent decision variables and edges represent constraint relationships. These problem-agnostic graphs guide our structure-based local improvement framework (SLIM) in variable selection. Meanwhile, the weighted graph enables all problem instances to share the same generic graph representation, from which the same graph features can be extracted and used for configuration selection. We evaluated our pipeline on instances across 20 MiniZinc competition problems, finding that algorithm selection achieves a 39.5% average problem-weighted win rate against a one-shot Gurobi baseline, more than doubling the best single configuration (19.3%). Configuration and feature ablation boost the performance further to 44.0%, demonstrating that LLM-based semantic generation enables effective automated structure extraction and feature extraction for constraint optimization.

</details>


### [145] [LongEarth-R1: Benchmarking and Aligning Vision-Language Models for Long-Horizon Earth Observation Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13344)
*Yupan Ding,Jing Xiao,Zhenyuan Zhang,Chaofeng Chen,Liang Liao,Gui-Song Xia,Mi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LongEarth-Bench基准（约12万问答样本，来自11.7万张图像，序列平均15.14帧、最长30帧，覆盖12项长时序遥感推理任务），并开发了LongEarth模型（通过监督微调+结构化思维链）及LongEarth-R1（引入GRPO策略优化与格式/时序/空间奖励），在12项长序列任务上全面最优，同时保持标准遥感基准的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感视觉语言模型主要聚焦于孤立图像、图像对或短序列，难以支撑长时序地表观测推理所需的跨多阶段地理演化组织、空间变化定位、时间异常检测及基于扩展影像序列的未来推断，缺乏可靠的帧级和区域级grounding能力。

Method: 1) 构建LongEarth-Bench基准：约12万问答样本、11.7万唯一图像，序列平均15.14帧、最长30帧，覆盖演化总结、空间推理、异常识别、逻辑预测四大类共12项任务；其中3万样本子集提供结构化推理轨迹（关键帧+变化区域→最终答案）。2) 提出LongEarth模型：通过带显式序列标识符的监督微调与结构化思维链监督训练。3) 提出LongEarth-R1：在LongEarth基础上应用组相对策略优化（GRPO），引入格式奖励、时序奖励和空间奖励。

Result: LongEarth-R1在全部12项长序列任务上取得最优结果，同时在与现有方法的标准遥感基准对比中仍保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过大规模长序列基准、结构化推理轨迹监督以及结合时序和空间奖励的强化学习，可显著提升遥感视觉语言模型的长时序推理与可靠grounding能力，为长视野地表观测推理提供了新的前沿方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon Earth observation reasoning requires models to organize multi-stage geographic evolution, localize spatial changes, detect temporal anomalies, and infer future from extended image sequences. However, existing remote sensing vision-language models mainly focus on isolated images, image pairs, or short sequences, limiting reliable grounding in the relevant frames and regions. We introduce LongEarth-Bench, a benchmark containing approximately 120k question-answering samples derived from 117k unique images. Its sequences average 15.14 frames and extend to 30 frames, covering 12 tasks across evolution summarization, spatial reasoning, anomaly identification, and logical prediction. A 30k-sample subset further provides structured reasoning traces linking key frames and changed regions to final answers. We develop LongEarth through supervised fine-tuning with explicit sequence identifiers and structured chain-of-thought supervision. Building on LongEarth, LongEarth-R1 applies group relative policy optimization with format, temporal, and spatial rewards. LongEarth-R1 achieves the best results on all 12 long-sequence tasks while remaining competitive on standard remote sensing benchmarks.

</details>


### [146] [Rules or Character? Scaling Laws for AI Safety Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13345)
*Satoshi Takahashi,Nobuji Kouno,Masaaki Komatsu,Ryuji Hamamoto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个人工智能安全系统的比较静态模型，将安全设计参数化为性格塑造与规则执行之间的资源分配alpha，并分析了在部署规模增长时最优安全架构的演变规律。研究发现最优alpha*随部署规模T增大而向性格塑造方向移动，但主导因素并非规模本身，而是性格塑造在分布偏移下的脆弱性基线率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI安全系统通常结合两种方法：训练时的性格塑造（如RLHF、宪政AI）和推理时的规则执行（如输出过滤器、安全分类器），但现有研究缺乏对这两者最优平衡如何随部署规模扩大而变化的正式分析。

Method: 构建了一个风格化的比较静态模型，将安全设计参数化为[0,1]区间内的资源分配alpha，纳入尺度相关的过滤器退化、共因失效和性格脆弱性等因素。在乘法Pareto损害模型下推导出期望危害的闭式解，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟补充尾部风险（CVaR）分析。

Result: 在乐观、中等、悲观三种场景下，最优alpha*位于内部点或规则执行边界，并随部署规模T增大而向性格塑造方向弱移动（Delta alpha*从+0.01到+0.21不等）。主导参数是基线性格脆弱率p^(0)_frag，其跨全范围可使alpha*移动0.50，远超尾部严重性、过滤器质量或共因失效概率的影响。CVaR与期望危害最优点在大规模T下收敛。

Conclusion: 安全架构决策更多取决于性格塑造在分布偏移下的可靠性，而非部署规模本身，这为AI安全系统的资源分配提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) safety systems combine character shaping (e.g., Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback [RLHF], Constitutional AI), which modifies behavioral distributions at training time, with rule enforcement (e.g., output filters, safety classifiers), which blocks harmful outputs at inference time, yet little formal analysis exists on how their optimal balance should change as deployment scales increase. We introduce a stylized comparative-statics model that parameterizes safety design as a resource allocation alpha in [0,1] between these two approaches, incorporating scale-dependent filter degradation, common-mode failures, and character fragility -- the risk that shaped behavior degrades or collapses under novel conditions. Under a multiplicative Pareto damage model, we derive closed-form expected harm and supplement it with tail-risk (CVaR) analysis via Monte Carlo simulation. Across three scenarios (optimistic, moderate, pessimistic), the optimal alpha* is interior or at the rules-only boundary and shifts weakly toward character shaping as deployment scale T grows, from negligible (Delta alpha* = +0.01) to pronounced (Delta alpha* = +0.21) depending on scenario. The dominant parameter is the baseline character fragility rate p^(0)_frag, which shifts alpha* by 0.50 across its range -- far exceeding the effect of tail severity, filter quality, or common-mode failure probability. CVaR and expected-harm optima converge at large T. These results suggest that safety architecture decisions depend less on deployment scale per se than on the reliability of character shaping under distributional shift.

</details>


### [147] [Jointly Predicting Courses and Grades Using a Transformer-Based Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13409)
*Paul Savala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE（Transformer for Academic Course-grade Estimation）模型，将学生学术历史视为每学期并修课程的结构化数据而非简单序列，同时联合预测下一学期的课程集合及对应成绩。在十年院校数据上，该联合预测模型相比仅预测成绩的模型将平均绝对误差降低近50%，并优于LSTM序列模型和图神经网络方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习分析中的预测模型将学生学术历史视为简单序列，忽视了同一学期内课程并修（concurrency）的特性。这种简化导致对课业负担重或课程难度高的学生的成绩预测不准确。

Method: 提出TRACE模型：1）以学期为单位编码课程，捕捉课程并修效应；2）设计联合损失函数，同时优化课程集合预测与成绩预测；3）利用Transformer架构实现；4）支持融合学生属性数据；5）训练数据为十年院校历史数据。

Result: 联合预测模型比同架构仅预测成绩的模型减少近50%的平均绝对误差；优于传统LSTM序列模型和图神经网络方法；模型可通过重训练和重新校准适配新院校。

Conclusion: 工作证明了现代神经架构在创建可解释学习预测模型方面的效用，强调了预测课程集合这一关键训练技巧的重要性，并讨论了将模型整合到高等教育早期检测系统中的可行性。

Abstract: Existing predictive models in learning analytics often treat student academic history as a simple sequence, overlooking the concurrent nature of courses taken within a semester. This simplification can lead to inaccurate performance predictions, particularly for students with heavy or challenging course loads. This paper introduces a TRansformer for Academic Course-grade Estimation (TRACE) that addresses this limitation by jointly predicting both the set of courses a student will take and their corresponding grades for an upcoming semester. Our approach encodes courses on a per-semester basis to capture the effects of course concurrency and utilizes a novel loss function combining course-set prediction with grade prediction. We demonstrate that predicting courses taken in addition to the grades in those courses leads to significant improvements in prediction quality. Trained on ten years of institutional data, our joint prediction model reduces mean absolute error by nearly 50% compared to an identical architecture that predicts grades alone. The model also outperforms traditional LSTM-based sequential models, as well as graph neural network-based approaches, and offers natural ways to incorporate student attribute data. This work demonstrates the utility of modern neural architectures for creating interpretable models that can be adapted to new institutions via retraining and recalibration, as well as the importance of key techniques, such as predicting courses taken during training. We discuss how this model could be incorporated into early detection systems at institutions of higher education.

</details>


### [148] [Who Speaks Matters: Authority-Aware Multi-View RAG over Italian Parliamentary Proceedings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13410)
*Mirko Tritella,Riccardo Pozzi,Matteo Palmonari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ParliamentRAG，一个面向意大利众议院议会记录的知识增强生成系统，通过主题相关的发言人权威模型来解决RAG在议会场景中的三大风险：最频繁发言者主导、无法按议题专长加权、政治敏感文本中的引文错误归属。系统在15个政策主题上与Google NotebookLM对比评估，在跨党派覆盖率和引文忠实度上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 议会程序是民主审议的主要记录，但其体量和碎片化使得公民、记者和研究者难以从多视角获取信息。将RAG应用于议会记录存在三个具体风险：频繁发言者主导检索结果、系统缺乏按议题专长对发言人加权的机制、政治敏感文本中的引用归属错误。这些风险共同阻碍了系统在议会场景中的可靠应用。

Method: ParliamentRAG的核心贡献是构建了一个主题相关的权威（authority）模型，该模型根据当前查询动态估计每位发言人的权威度，融合了职业、教育背景和过往发言等可解释组件。系统流程为：根据用户查询检索相关发言片段，跨议会党团识别议题相关专家，生成综合各方视角的摘要并附支持性引文。评估采用两层级协议，结合自动化指标和六位领域专家的盲审A/B人工评估，在15个政策主题上与Google NotebookLM对比。

Result: ParliamentRAG在跨政治党团覆盖率（0.97 vs 0.95）和引文忠实度（1.00 vs 0.95）上优于Google NotebookLM，专家在来源相关维度上更偏好该系统；而NotebookLM在散文质量相关维度上表现更强。

Conclusion: ParliamentRAG有效缓解了议会RAG场景中的发言者主导偏差、议题专长加权和引文归属三大问题，验证了主题相关权威模型在本场景中的价值。系统在信息来源的可信度和忠实性方面优于商业产品基线，但在文本生成质量上仍有改进空间，未来可探索融合两者优势的方向。

Abstract: Parliamentary proceedings are a primary record of democratic deliberation, yet their volume and fragmentation make multi-perspective access difficult for citizens, journalists, and researchers. Applying Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to parliamentary transcripts introduces three specific risks: dominance of the most frequent speakers, inability to weight speakers according to topical expertise, and citation misattribution in politically sensitive text. We present ParliamentRAG, a RAG system for the Italian Chamber of Deputies that addresses these risks jointly. Its core contribution is a topic-dependent authority model that estimates each speaker's authority as a function of the current query, combining interpretable components such as profession, education, and previous interventions. Given a user query, the system retrieves relevant speech chunks, identifies topic-relevant experts across parliamentary groups, and generates a summary synthesizing their perspectives, accompanied by supporting quotations. ParliamentRAG is evaluated against Google NotebookLM on 15 policy topics via a two-level protocol combining automated metrics and blind A/B human evaluation by six domain experts. The system achieves higher coverage across political groups (0.97 vs. 0.95), perfect quotation faithfulness (1.00 vs. 0.95), and stronger expert preferences on source-related dimensions, while NotebookLM remains stronger on prose-oriented dimensions.

</details>


### [149] [Beyond Final Scores: A Systematic Evaluation of Agents for Long-Horizon AI Research and Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13417)
*Yiwei Li,Wanli Yang,Hexiang Tan,Xiangzhou Huang,Zhengyu Chen,Ziran Li,Borun Chen,Shanglin Lei,Huaisheng Zhu,Hao Tian,Fei Sun,Xunliang Cai,Jingang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对七个前沿模型在36个长时程任务上进行了系统性评估，提出了一个利用基于规则指标刻画运行中行为（问题构架、执行、反馈控制）的新框架，并通过受控比较评估经验复用。结果显示当前智能体更类似于工程优化器而非完全自主的研究者：能制定和实现实用方案，但运行间性能差异大，最强方案主要改编或组合已有技术，真正的方法论创新仍然罕见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估仅关注最终分数，既无法揭示进度在何处获得或丢失，也无法判断累积经验是否能改善后续决策。因此需要超越最终分数的系统性评估框架，以理解自主智能体在长时程实验中的真实能力状态。

Method: 提出一个新评估框架：（1）使用基于规则（rule-based）的指标，通过方案构架（Solution Framing）、执行（Execution）、反馈控制（Feedback Control）三个维度刻画运行内（within-run）行为；（2）通过受控比较评估任务内与跨任务的经验复用（experience reuse）。基于该框架对七个前沿模型在36个长时程任务上进行系统性评估。

Result: 当前智能体表现更像工程优化器而非完全自主的研究者。具体发现：能制定并实现实用解决方案，但运行间性能波动显著；最强方案主要依赖改编或组合已有技术；真正的方法论新颖性罕见。详细分析显示性能受多重因素影响：相似最终结果背后存在不同的过程瓶颈、经验复用既可能帮助也可能误导后续决策、框架（harness）设计影响性能稳定性。

Conclusion: 研究为改进模型训练、推理时策略、经验管理和框架设计提供了具体方向，表明需要从仅关注最终得分转向对运行过程与经验积累的系统性评估与优化。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly capable of improving models, systems, and other technical artifacts through long-horizon experimentation. To understand the current state of this capability, however, evaluation must go beyond final scores, which neither reveal where progress is gained or lost nor indicate whether accumulated experience improves later decisions. We therefore present a systematic evaluation of seven frontier models on 36 long-horizon tasks based on a new framework that uses rule-based metrics to characterize within-run behavior through Solution Framing, Execution, and Feedback Control and controlled comparisons to assess experience reuse within and across tasks. The results show that current agents operate more like engineering optimizers than fully autonomous researchers: they can formulate and implement practical solutions, but their performance varies substantially across runs, their strongest solutions mainly adapt or combine established techniques, and genuine methodological novelty remains rare. Detailed analysis reveals that observed performance is shaped by multiple factors, including distinct process bottlenecks behind similar final outcomes, experience reuse that can help or mislead subsequent decisions, and harness designs that affect performance stability. These findings suggest concrete directions for improving model training, inference-time strategies, experience management, and harness design.

</details>


### [150] [MARC v1: An Open-Source Multi-Agent Framework for Clinical AI Reasoning and Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13476)
*Saisha Shetty,Satvik Tripathi,Austin Lin,Colin Zhao,Theodore Kim,Don Enwerem,Jacinta Arnold,Shahriar Faghani,Tessa S Cook*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MARC是一个开源的临床推理多智能体框架，用确定性多智能体编排取代单一大语言模型提示，包含提取、推理、答案生成和评估等角色专用智能体，并引入Decomposer模块自动生成任务特定提示，支持API和本地CPU部署，无需编程经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单一LLM提示在临床推理中缺乏可解释性和失败归因能力，且手动提示工程耗时且需要专业知识，临床领域专家难以直接使用。

Method: 采用确定性多智能体编排框架，协调提取、推理、答案生成和评估四个角色专用智能体，通过显式上下文传递和可追踪中间输出实现阶段级失败归因；引入Decomposer模块从自然语言描述自动生成任务特定智能体提示；支持API和本地CPU部署，通过YAML完全配置。

Result: 提供了一个模型无关、可解释、临床领域专家无需编程即可使用的开源框架，支持阶段级失败归因和YAML零代码配置。

Conclusion: MARC通过确定性多智能体编排和自动提示生成，提高了临床推理的可解释性、可访问性和可部署性

Abstract: We present Multi-Agent Reasoning and Coordination (MARC), an open-source framework that replaces monolithic LLM prompting with deterministic multi-agent orchestration for clinical reasoning. MARC coordinates role-specialized agents for extraction, reasoning, answer generation, and evaluation, with explicit context passing and traceable intermediate outputs, enabling stage-wise failure attribution. We additionally introduce a Decomposer module that generates task-specific agent prompts from a plain-language description, eliminating manual prompt engineering. The framework supports both API-based and local CPU-compatible deployments and is entirely configurable via YAML, without code modifications. MARC is designed to be model-agnostic, interpretable, and accessible to clinical domain experts without programming expertise. The full framework is available at https://github.com/Penn-RAIL/MARC-v1.

</details>


### [151] [Enhancing Virtual Agents through SLMs and Edge-Computing: An Exploratory Evaluation of Think and Memory Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13420)
*Aimilios Hadjiliasi,Louis Nisiotis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索使用小型语言模型（SLMs）在边缘计算环境中支持具身智能虚拟代理的认知组件运行，基于认知具身代理架构（CEAA）的"Think"和"Memory"过程，在NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX上开发并评估了一种边缘虚拟代理网关系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能虚拟代理需要在复杂的虚拟世界和Metaverse中作为持久、自适应且上下文感知的实体运行，但在这些环境中实现具有认知能力的代理在概念上和技术上都极具挑战性；同时边缘计算和生成式AI语言模型的进展为在资源受限的边缘设备上支持代理认知操作提供了新的可能。

Method: 基于CEAA框架（涵盖感知、记忆、推理、规划与具身行动等组件），聚焦"Think"和"Memory"两个核心认知过程，采用Qwen2.5系列不同规模的小型语言模型，在NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX边缘设备上构建并实现了边缘虚拟代理网关系统，通过模拟实验评估路由准确性、记忆读取性能和延迟。

Result: 一系列模拟实验评估了路由准确性、记忆读取性能和延迟，结果表明该系统是一个由SLM驱动的原型代理系统，能够部分实现CEAA的选定认知过程，支持具身代理的认知"大脑"在沉浸式虚拟世界中高效且上下文相关地运行。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了小型语言模型结合边缘计算可以有效支撑具身虚拟代理的部分认知功能，为在交互式虚拟世界中开发具有持久性和上下文感知能力的具身代理提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Embodied intelligent virtual agents are expected to operate as persistent, adaptive, and context-aware entities within complex virtual and Metaverse worlds. However, implementing cognitively capable agents in such environments is conceptually and technologically challenging. Among a range of blueprints and development approaches, the Cognitive Embodied Agent Architecture (CEAA) has been developed as an implementation-oriented framework for architecting components of perception, memory, reasoning, planning, and embodied action. Considering the recent advances in edge computing and generative AI language models, this paper explores the use of Small Language Models (SLMs) to support edge-based operation of selected CEAA components, focusing on "Think" and "Memory" as processes central to cognitive orchestration and persistence of virtual agents in interactive virtual worlds. An edge-based virtual agent gateway system was developed and evaluated on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX using Qwen2.5 models of different sizes, exploring the system's capability to process service requests and handle memory-driven conversations. A series of simulation experiments evaluated routing accuracy, memory-read performance, and latency, demonstrating an SLM-driven prototype agent system that partially implements selected CEAA processes to support the development of embodied agents whose cognitive "brain" can operate efficiently and contextually for interactive experiences in immersive virtual worlds.

</details>


### [152] [OmniScientist: An Omni-Modal Omni-Discipline AI Scientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13558)
*Bobo Li,Hao Fei,Tianjie Ju,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniScientist，一个端到端的全模态AI科学家系统，能够直接从异构原始证据（图像、信号、音频、视频、3D结构、轨迹、表格、公式和图）进行多学科研究。系统包含感知层和三个自治智能体（构思、实验、写作），在确定性流水线中运行，通过代码级检查强制执行新颖性筛查、统计有效性、执行来源和数值可追溯性。在36个真实数据案例中全部完成从原始数据到编译稿件的完整流程，平均论文评分6.3，相较于仅接收预计算标量特征的盲变体在所有7个评估维度上更优，赢得85%的头对头判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI科学家系统虽能覆盖研究流程（假设生成、代码执行、稿件撰写），但仅能推理文本、代码、标签或预计算摘要，无法访问科学发现所依赖的时空、跨通道和过程性关系等关键证据。作者认为工作流覆盖本身不足以提供完整证据访问，需要直接从异构原始证据进行全模态感知。

Method: OmniScientist采用确定性流水线架构，包含：(1) 感知层处理多种模态的原始证据；(2) 三个自治智能体分别负责构思（ideation）、实验（experiment）和写作（writeup），使观测结果能够影响研究问题、实验决策和最终结论；(3) 在代码层面执行创新性检查、严谨性检查和声明检查，强制保证新颖性筛查、统计有效性、执行来源追踪和数值可追溯性。

Result: 在36个真实数据案例（覆盖5个学科家族、4类科学证据家族，涉及图像、信号、音频、视频、3D结构、轨迹、表格、公式和图等模态）上，系统在全部36个案例中完成从原始数据到编译稿件的完整路径，使用参考推理骨干时平均论文评分达6.3。与仅接收预计算标量特征的盲变体相比，直接感知在所有7个评估维度上均更优，并在85%的头对头比较中获胜。

Conclusion: 结果表明，贯穿研究生命周期（lifecycle-wide）的感知对于基于证据的科学研究发现至关重要，为构建广泛能力的AI科学家提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in foundation models have enabled AI scientists to automate increasingly complete research workflows, from hypothesis generation and code execution to manuscript preparation. Yet workflow coverage alone does not provide access to the full evidence on which scientific discovery depends. Existing systems typically reason over text, code, labels, or precomputed summaries, leaving scientifically decisive spatial, temporal, cross-channel, and procedural relations unavailable to the agent. We introduce OmniScientist, an end-to-end, omni-modal AI scientist that conducts multidisciplinary research directly from heterogeneous raw evidence. A perception layer and 3 autonomous agents for ideation, experiment, and writeup operate within a deterministic pipeline, allowing observations to shape research questions, experimental decisions, and final claims throughout the research lifecycle. By running idea, rigour, and claim checks in code, the system enforces novelty screening, statistical validity, execution provenance, and numerical traceability. We evaluate OmniScientist on 36 real-data cases spanning 5 discipline families, 4 families of scientific evidence, and modalities including images, signals, audio, video, 3-D structures, trajectories, tables, formulae, and graphs. The system completes the full path from raw data to a compiled manuscript in all 36 cases and achieves a mean overall paper score of 6.3 with the reference reasoning backbone. In paired comparisons against a blind variant that receives only precomputed scalar features, direct perception improves all 7 evaluation dimensions and wins 85% of head-to-head judgments. These results show that lifecycle-wide perception is essential for evidence-grounded scientific discovery and provides a practical path toward broadly capable AI scientists.

</details>


### [153] [RAIL: An Automatic Classifier of the Artificial Intelligence Readiness Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13428)
*Juan Irving Vasquez,Juan Terven,Laura-Ivoone Garay-Jimenez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出统一AI就绪度水平（AIRL）九级序数量表，基于环境证据阶梯和维度上限，使就绪度可从自然语言描述直接判定；并提出RAIL多LLM专家面板分类器实现该量表的自动化评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI技术成熟度评估框架异构且难以自动应用：TRL缺乏AI特定门控标准、MLTRL依赖内部过程工件、AI/数据就绪度维度模型量表难以直接比较，亟需统一的、可自动判定的就绪度评估框架。

Method: 提出两个贡献：(1) AIRL统一量表——九级序数尺度，基于环境证据阶梯，辅以六项维度上限（规格、数据存在性、数据质量、数据合法性、专家知识、算法成熟度）、通用性锚定规则和明确的赋值纪律，使就绪度仅凭自然语言描述即可判定；(2) RAIL分类器——由1个证据代理和6个独立维度代理（均为窄范围职责的LLM）组成的专家面板，通过确定性最小规则聚合，再由首席专家在不对称权限下审核（只能确认或降低、不得超过上限提升）。

Result: 该方法在多项研究工作的分析中进行了测试，结果表明其具有一致性，且避免了单体LLM分类器的高估问题。

Conclusion: AIRL与RAIL组合提供了一种可判定、自动化、防高估的AI技术就绪度评估方法，兼顾多样性与审核安全机制，为投资决策和项目管理提供了更可靠的支撑。

Abstract: Assessing the maturity of artificial intelligence technologies is essential for investment decisions, project management, and policy monitoring, yet the available readiness frameworks are heterogeneous and difficult to apply automatically: the adaptation of Technology Readiness Levels to AI lacks AI-specific gating criteria, the Machine Learning Technology Readiness Levels presuppose access to internal process artifacts, and AI/data readiness dimension models employ scales that resist direct comparison. This paper makes two contributions. First, we unify these three frameworks into the Unified AI Readiness Level (AIRL), a nine-level ordinal scale built on an environmental evidence ladder and complemented by dimensional caps (covering specification, data existence, data quality, data legality, expert knowledge, and algorithmic maturity) together with a generality-anchoring rule and explicit assignment disciplines, so that a readiness level becomes decidable from a natural-language description of the work alone. Second, we propose RAIL (Readiness Assessment via Independent LLM-experts), a panel-of-experts classifier that operationalizes the scale: one evidence agent and six independent dimension agents, each a large language model with a narrowly scoped mandate, deliver verdicts that a deterministic minimum rule aggregates and a chief expert reviews under asymmetric authority, confirming or lowering the panel's recommendation but never raising it above the caps. The method was tested in the analysis of several research works showing consistency and avoiding overestimation from monolithic LLM classifiers.

</details>


### [154] [Academic League of Artificial Intelligence - An Integrative Perspective of Teaching, Research, and Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13447)
*Alison R. Panisson,Maria Eduarda W. M. Vianna,Italo Firmino da Silva,Heitor Henrique da Silva,Rafaela Fernandes Savaris,Bernardo Pandolfi Costa,Martin Augusto Gagliotti Vigil,Jim Lau,Agenor Hentz,Andréa Sabedra Bordin,Alexandre Leopoldo Gonçalves,Roberto Rodrigues-Filho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了巴西圣卡塔琳娜联邦大学人工智能学术联盟（LIA）所采用的组织框架，该框架以学生为中心、基于项目导向，通过民主治理、协作学习和动态项目组织，整合教学、科研和大学延伸三大支柱，以促进技术和横向能力的发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术联盟已成为促进课外教育和加强大学与社会融合的重要机制，但缺乏系统性的组织框架指导，需要提出一种可复制、灵活的模型来整合教学、科研和大学延伸三大支柱。

Method: 提出一个结合民主治理、协作学习和动态项目组织的组织框架，并通过竞赛团队、学习小组、公开讲座、知识库和具有社会影响力的人工智能应用等代表性项目进行阐述和验证。

Result: 该框架成功支持了多样化的教育、科研和延伸活动在统一组织结构中开展，促进了领导力培养、科学产出、社区参与和知识保存。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为工程和计算教育中整合大学三大支柱提供了灵活且可复制的模型，可为学术联盟和类似学生组织提供实践指导。

Abstract: Academic leagues have become important mechanisms for promoting extracurricular education and strengthening the integration between universities and society. This paper presents the organizational framework adopted by the Academic League of Artificial Intelligence (LIA) at the Federal University of Santa Catarina (UFSC), designed to integrate teaching, research, and university extension through a student-centered, project-based approach. The framework combines democratic governance, collaborative learning, and dynamic project organization to foster both technical and transversal competencies. The framework is illustrated through representative initiatives, including competition teams, study groups, open lectures, knowledge repositories, and AI-powered applications with social impact. These projects demonstrate how diverse educational, scientific, and extension activities can be developed within a common organizational structure while promoting leadership, scientific production, community engagement, and knowledge preservation. The reported experience indicates that the proposed framework provides a flexible and replicable model for integrating the three university pillars into engineering and computing education, offering practical guidance for academic leagues and similar student organizations.

</details>


### [155] [A Unifying Perspective on Causal World Models: From Observations to Representations to Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13456)
*Avinash Kori,Fabrizio Russo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从因果视角系统性地研究世界模型（WM），提出因果世界模型（CWM）的形式化定义，强调实用的世界模型不应仅具备生成能力，还需捕捉实体属性、实体间交互以及实体与环境间的交互，并将其与因果表示学习、目标中心学习、因果发现、结构因果模型和基于模型的决策等既有工作联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型主要关注生成能力（如预测和规划），但缺乏对环境中决定动态过程的因果结构的显式建模，导致模型难以在分布外泛化并支持因果推理和理性决策。作者认为需要从因果视角重新审视世界模型，明确其应捕获的结构与表示。

Method: 从多个抽象层次（从感知观察到环境动态的概念结构）研究世界模型，提出Causal WMs（CWMs）的形式化定义，并连接因果表示学习、目标中心学习、因果发现、结构因果模型和基于模型的决策等研究方向；还讨论了可辨识性（identifiability）条件，澄清WM组件何时可从数据中恢复及其等价程度。

Result: 给出了因果世界模型的正式定义框架，建立了世界模型与因果相关研究领域之间的理论联系，并阐明了可辨识性条件下各组件可被恢复的程度，从而以支持因果推理和明智决策的表示与结构来夯实世界模型的理论基础。

Conclusion: 实用的世界模型必须超越生成能力，捕获实体属性及其交互所构成的因果结构；通过形式化定义和可辨识性分析，因果世界模型能够为智能体在分布外进行预测、规划和行动提供坚实基础，是未来智能体研究的重要方向。

Abstract: World Models (WM) are increasingly seen as a foundation for intelligent agents that can predict, plan, and act beyond their training distribution. In this paper, we study WMs from a causal perspective across multiple levels of abstraction, ranging from perceptual observations to building a conceptual representation of the structure governing the environment dynamics. We argue that useful WMs must go beyond generative capabilities alone: they should also capture entity properties, entity-to-entity interactions, and entity-to-environment interactions that determine and explain the dynamics of a system. We provide a formal definition of Causal WMs (CWMs) grounded in the tasks they are intended to support, connecting world modelling with existing work in causal representation learning, object-centric learning, causal discovery, structural causal models, and model-based decision-making. Finally, we relate CWMs to the literature on identifiability, clarifying when the components of a WM can be recovered from data and up to which equivalence. With this, we ground WMs in representations and structures that support causal reasoning and informed decision-making.

</details>


### [156] [AlayaWorld: Interactive Long-Horizon World Modeling - Full Technical Report (v1.1)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13492)
*AlayaWorld Team,Kaipeng Zhang,Chuanhao Li,Yifan Zhan,Yongtao Ge,Yuanyang Yin,Jiaming Tan,Kang He,Liaoyuan Fan,Mingliang Zhai,Ruicong Liu,Xiaojie Xu,Xuangeng Chu,Zhen Li,Zhengyuan Lin,Zhixiang Wang,Zian Meng,Zihui Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AlayaWorld的改进版本，其核心原则是让条件信号在潜在表示和时间结构上尽可能与生成内容匹配。通过六项关键修改（包括以流式3D点缓存渲染器替代深度扭曲空间记忆、在相同因果VAE潜在空间编码视觉条件等），显著改进了空间记忆与条件信号的处理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前版本的AlayaWorld中，条件信号的表征方式与生成内容在潜在表示和时间结构上存在不一致，导致条件信息与生成视频之间的对齐不够紧密。作者认为，要让条件信号更有效地引导视频生成，条件应尽量在潜在空间和时间结构上贴近生成内容。

Method: 提出了六项修改：（1）用运动感知潜在条件替换静态帧图像条件；（2）将重新渲染的空间记忆按因果方式编码为连续序列；（3）在像素空间对齐时间记忆窗口；（4）采用硬记忆丢弃（移除记忆token而非置零）；（5）统一训练和推理时的VAE编解码协议；（6）移除相机AdaLN分支，使视角控制完全由重渲染的空间条件提供。模型采用流式3D点缓存渲染器替代之前的深度扭曲空间记忆。

Result: 该报告未在摘要中提供定量实验结果，但描述了改进方案的核心设计：新的设计遵循"条件信号应与生成内容在潜在表征和时间结构上尽可能匹配"这一原则，取代了基于深度扭曲的空间记忆方案，并统一了条件编码与生成内容的潜在空间。

Conclusion: AlayaWorld的改进版本通过全面重构条件信号的表示与集成方式，使得条件信息在潜在空间和时间维度上与生成内容高度一致，从而提升了模型对空间记忆和视角控制的使用效率。

Abstract: This report presents an improved version of AlayaWorld. While the backbone architecture, chunk-wise autoregressive generation scheme, and training data remain unchanged from the previous release, we substantially revise how conditioning signals are represented and integrated into the model. The new design is guided by a simple principle: conditioning signals should match the generated content as closely as possible in both latent representation and temporal structure. To this end, we make two major changes. First, we replace the previous depth-warping-based spatial memory with a streaming 3D point-cache renderer. Second, we redesign the conditioning pipeline so that visual conditions are encoded in the same causal-VAE latent space, with temporal statistics consistent with those of the generated video. Concretely, the new version introduces six modifications: (1) replacing static-frame image conditioning with motion-aware latent conditioning; (2) causally encoding re-rendered spatial memory as a continuous sequence; (3) aligning the temporal-memory window in pixel space; (4) adopting hard memory dropout that removes memory tokens rather than zeroing them; (5) unifying the VAE encoding and decoding protocol across training and inference; and (6) removing the camera AdaLN branch, such that viewpoint control is provided entirely through the re-rendered spatial condition.

</details>


### [157] [QuoteBench: How Matched Scores Can Hide Command-Path Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13547)
*Shangao Li,Yao Zhang,Volker Tresp,Yuanyuan Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QuoteBench基准，通过精确最终状态验证和刻意引入未转义解析器干扰，测量LLM编码代理在生成命令与执行传输之间的性能裂隙，证明匹配执行分数无法区分生成错误与执行层引入的失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码代理通过接口发出Bash命令，接口会对模型输出进行序列化、包装和重新解析。匹配执行分数无法区分命令生成错误与生成后引入的失败，这导致对模型能力的评估存在系统性混淆。

Method: QuoteBench使用14个事件衍生的56个一次性任务，采用精确最终状态验证，交叉生成契约与执行传输（围绕一个刻意未转义的解析器）。使用8种同窗口配置进行重放实验，测量同一回复在加入解析器后成功率的变化及披露边界后的恢复能力。

Result: 加入解析器后成功率降低55.4至73.2个百分点；6种配置在披露后恢复30.4至60.7个百分点，2种配置恢复为零或略负。原始生成近乎饱和，边界适应能力仍区分模型。GPT-5.6-sol的匹配缺口-3.6点掩盖了-64.3点损伤和+60.7点补偿。部署配置使模型排名重排：26对可比模型中1对明确反转，4对在单任务边缘。

Conclusion: 评估命令发出代理时应报告模型配置、生成契约、执行路径、操作点和最终状态验证器，而不是将匹配分数视为模型的内在属性。边界适应能力是当前模型之间真正的区分维度。

Abstract: LLM coding agents issue Bash commands through interfaces that may serialize, wrap, and reparse model output. Matched execution scores alone cannot distinguish command-generation errors from failures introduced after generation. QuoteBench measures this boundary with exact final-state validation on 56 one-shot tasks from 14 incident-derived families, crossing the generation contract with the execution transport around one deliberately unescaped added parser. Escaping at the interpolation point reproduces each replayed reply's raw-path outcome, so any recovery under a disclosed boundary must come from the model changing its generation. Across eight same-window configurations, replaying the same reply through the added parser lowers success by 55.4 to 73.2 percentage points; disclosure recovers 30.4 to 60.7 points for six configurations, and zero or slightly negative for the other two. Raw generation is nearly saturated at the frontier; boundary adaptation is what still separates models. GPT-5.6-sol's matched gap of -3.6 points hides -64.3 points of damage and +60.7 points of compensation. The deployment configuration reorders models: one reversal among 26 comparable pairs is unambiguous and four more sit on single-task margins. Evaluations of command-issuing agents should report the model configuration, generation contract, execution path, operating point, and final-state validator rather than treat a matched score as an intrinsic model property.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [DCBA: Detection of Collaborative Black-Hole Attacks in Connected Dominated Set using Baiting Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12347)
*Lata B T,Venugopal K R*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的协作型黑洞攻击检测与隔离技术（LW-CBH），通过增强AODV路由协议，利用基于定时器的诱饵机制和反向追踪过程，结合MAC层的控制状态消息（R-SEQ和C-SEQ）来检测并隔离MANET中的协作型黑洞攻击节点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MANET由于缺乏集中管理和动态拓扑特性，极易遭受各类安全攻击。协作型黑洞攻击中多个恶意节点相互配合，严重降低网络可靠性。现有的AODV路由协议无法在动态拓扑变化中有效检测恶意节点，因此需要一种轻量级的检测机制来识别和隔离协作型黑洞攻击节点。

Method: 提出LW-CBH（Lightweight Collaborative Black-Hole detection）方案：1）增强现有AODV路由协议；2）采用基于定时器的诱饵（baiting）过程；3）利用反向追踪（reverse tracing）设置来定位恶意节点；4）通过MAC层的控制状态消息——Reply Sequence（R-SEQ）和Code Sequence（C-SEQ）消息对连通支配集（connected dominated set）节点进行检测；5）在NS-2.35离散事件模拟器上实现仿真。

Result: 仿真使用NS-2.35工具，评估指标包括吞吐量、数据包投递率、平均端到端延迟和归一化路由开销，验证了所提LW-CBH方案在检测和隔离协作型黑洞攻击方面的有效性。

Conclusion: LW-CBH方案通过轻量级的诱饵与反向追踪机制，有效解决了MANET中协作型黑洞攻击的检测与隔离问题，弥补了现有AODV协议在动态拓扑下检测恶意节点的不足。

Abstract: Mobile Ad-hoc Network (MANET) is temporary and dynamic network topology, wherein nodes are mobile in nature and distributed randomly in a network area. In MANET, nodes cooperate with each other to operate and forward data through multihop communication between source and destination. MANET is exposed to different types of attacks due to absence of central administration. However, some nodes decline to cooperate, misbehaves and appears to be malicious affecting network functionality and connectivity. Providing security and identifying malicious node has become one of the challenging research topics in MANET. Black-hole attack is considered to be most popular attack that degrades the overall network performance. Black-hole node falsely advertises the shortest path to destination intentionally to disrupt the network communication resulting in packet drop. In collaborative black-hole attacks, multiple black-hole nodes cooperate and launch attacks in order to degrade network reliability. In this article we propose a Lightweight technique to detect and isolate Collaborative Black-Hole attacks (LW-CBH) by enhancing existing AODV routing protocol. In this scheme a timer based baiting process and reverse tracing setup is used to detect malicious node through control status message in MAC layer which are Reply Sequence (R-SEQ) and Code Sequence (C-SEQ) message of connected dominated set of nodes. However existing AODV routing protocol fails to detect malicious node during dynamic topology changing in MANET. Simulation of proposed technique is performed using discrete event simulator tool NS-2.35. The simulation results are evaluated for throughput, packet delivery ratio, average end-to-end delay and normalized routing overhead.

</details>


### [159] [The energetic cost of mitigating AI attacks in cellular networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12431)
*Adrián Losada,Hao Qiang Luo-Chen,David Segura,Carlos S. Alvarez-Merino,Milan Groshev,Emil J. Khatib,Raquel Barco*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了AI/ML技术在蜂窝网络（特别是O-RAN架构中的RIC）中应用所带来的安全漏洞与防御机制能耗成本之间的权衡，指出防御策略虽有效但会产生被忽视的计算能耗，并刻画了防御技术的能耗特征及ML精度、鲁棒性与能效三者之间的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI/ML算法在蜂窝网络各层面的深度集成（如O-RAN的RIC实现资源分配、负载均衡和能效优化），数据驱动的算法虽取得成功，但也引入了新的安全漏洞——攻击者可篡改数据属性使ML模型性能下降。现有缓解策略虽然有效，却产生了额外的计算负载和能耗成本，这一能耗在当前的节能背景下往往被忽视。因此，作者旨在揭示并刻画这一被忽略的能耗问题。

Method: 本文对一种防御技术的能耗进行了特征化（characterisation）研究，并系统性地概述了由ML精度（accuracy）、鲁棒性（robustness）和能效（energy efficiency）三者组成的三元组权衡带来的挑战。

Result: 研究刻画了防御技术的能耗特征，识别并阐明了ML精度、鲁棒性和能效三者之间存在的固有张力与权衡挑战。

Conclusion: 在安全防御的鲁棒性要求与网络能效目标之间存在不可忽视的矛盾，未来的蜂窝网络架构设计必须将防御机制的能耗纳入考量，以在ML精度、鲁棒性与能效之间寻求平衡。

Abstract: The integration of Artificial Intelligence (AI), generally as Machine Learning (ML) algorithms, in all levels and aspects of cellular networks demonstrates the success of data-driven algorithms; for example, the Radio Intelligence Controller (RIC) of the O-RAN paradigm bestows the network with optimised radio resource allocation, load balancing or energy efficiency functions, among others. Nevertheless, this dependency on data opens new security vulnerabilities, as attackers can alter data properties and steer ML models to underperform or degrade. Conversely, the developed mitigation strategies are effective, but they generate a computational load which, in consequence, results in an energy cost generally overlooked, even in the current energy-awareness context. In this work, consumption of a defence technique is characterised, and the challenges raised by the triad of ML accuracy, robustness and energy efficiency are outlined.

</details>


### [160] [Non-Degenerate Risk Certification for Automated Security Decisions: A Decision-Contract Theory with ATT\&CK-Aligned Triage as a Worked Instance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12444)
*Zhenpeng Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出决策契约理论（decision-contract theory），揭示任何风险证书都定义在决策契约之上，契约变弱会隐藏基分类器误差。基于误差守恒定律、无标签容量证书和非退化可行动性证书，解决了自动化决策风险界的空洞性问题（如全弃选方案），并在LLM入侵检测告警分流任务上实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化决策的"无条件下界风险界"存在结构性空洞：什么都不做的选择器即可使风险界为零，导致证书形同虚设。作者在LLM入侵检测告警分流场景中发现这一失效，需构建能排除空洞解并真正判定系统结构性容量的理论框架。

Method: 1) 提出决策契约概念（输入+语义正确关系），构建误差守恒定律（误差仅在有害自动化、人工推诿、语义遮蔽间重新分配）；2) 提出无标签单例容量证书，用于认证结构性无能力，并配合风险可行细化来区分可恢复的阈值错位与风险约束型无能力；3) 构造非退化可行动性证书，通过构造方式排除全弃选（all-abstain）方案。

Result: 在3个IDS数据集、6个LLM、4个错误率阈值下，90.3%的配置中经验性虚假归因风险不超目标，83.4%平均正确自动化率。容量诊断解释了所有低效用配置，细化区分了真实错位与风险约束无能力；训练稳定性重运行未发现确认的结构性无能力实例；细粒度攻击子类型标签验证了粗化-转移恒等式（coarsening-transfer identity），存在小但非零的遮蔽质量。

Conclusion: 自动化决策风险证书必须内嵌决策契约才能避免空洞性；误差守恒定律表明误差不可能消失只会转移；所提出的容量诊断与可行动性证书能有效识别和排除结构性问题，为高风险自动化决策提供可操作的认证框架。

Abstract: An unconditional risk bound on automated decisions can be satisfied without automating anything, since a selector that never acts drives the bound to zero. We show this is structural: any risk certificate is defined over a decision contract, the inputs a system acts on plus the semantic relation under which an output counts correct, and weakening either hides base-classifier error. We develop a decision-contract theory: an error-conservation law showing error is only reassigned among harmful automation, human deferral, and semantic masking; a label-free singleton capacity certifying structural incapacity, with a risk-feasible refinement separating recoverable threshold misalignment from risk-constrained incapacity; and a non-degenerate actionability certificate excluding all-abstain solutions by construction. We instantiate this on ATT\&CK-aligned alert triage for LLM-based intrusion detection, the setting that exposed the vacuity failure. Across 3 IDS datasets, 6 LLMs, and 4 error-rate thresholds, empirical false-attribution risk stays at or below target in 90.3% of configurations, with 83.4% mean correct automation. The capacity diagnostic explains every low-utility configuration; its refinement separates genuine misalignment from risk-constrained incapacity, confirmed by an exhibited alternative threshold; a training-stability re-run finds no confirmed structural-incapacity instance; and real fine-grained attack-subtype labels confirm the coarsening-transfer identity under a genuine many-to-one map, with small but non-zero masking mass.

</details>


### [161] [SoK: From Generation to Consumption of Privacy Documents in Software Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12511)
*Shidong Pan,Clark LaChance,Zhen Tao,Sepideh Ghanavati*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文献综述（SoK）从软件工程视角，对2010至2025年间发表的290篇关于隐私文档（如隐私政策、隐私标签等）的研究进行了系统性回顾，围绕五个研究问题组织分析，识别出15项关键研究趋势和21个开放机会，并提出四个未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私文档（如隐私政策）是数字服务披露数据实践和获取用户同意的核心机制。随着该领域研究快速扩展，涵盖传统隐私政策、简短通知（如隐私标签）及界面级透明度机制，研究者越来越难以获得关于隐私文档在生命周期中如何创建、分析、评估和维护的统一视角。因此需要一篇系统化的综述来整合这些分散的研究。

Method: 采用SoK（系统化知识）方法论，系统性地回顾并分析了2010至2025年间发表的290篇论文。论文围绕五个研究问题组织：(1) 隐私文档的定义与范围；(2) 文档的生成；(3) 分析与信息提取；(4) 不一致性与不合规性检查；(5) 可用性评估与改进。

Result: 识别出15项关键研究趋势和21个开放研究机会。此外，提出了四个更广泛的研究方向：AI中心平台带来的新兴挑战、对多样化和最新数据基础的需求、基于大语言模型的统一策略-代码分析，以及面向终端用户和开发者的双重可用性设计。

Conclusion: 该SoK提供了一个以生命周期为导向的隐私文档统一视角，为未来在隐私政策及相关隐私文档领域的研究奠定了共享基础，特别是在AI驱动的平台和LLM技术应用方面指出了重要的发展方向。

Abstract: Privacy documents (e.g., privacy policies) are a central mechanism through which digital services disclose data practices and seek user consent. Over the past decades, research on privacy documents has expanded significantly, encompassing not only traditional privacy policies but also short notices (e.g., privacy labels) and interface-level transparency mechanisms. As this research area continues to grow, it has become increasingly difficult to obtain a coherent view of how privacy documents are created, analyzed, evaluated, and maintained across their lifecycle. This SoK provides a unified, lifecycle-oriented view of privacy documents from a software engineering perspective. We systematically review and analyze 290 papers published between 2010 and 2025, organizing them around five research questions that examine how privacy documents are (1) defined and scoped, (2) generated, (3) analyzed and extracted, (4) checked for inconsistencies and noncompliance, and (5) evaluated and improved for usability. Building on our findings, we identify 15 key research trends and 21 open opportunities. We further chart four broader research directions that highlight (i) emerging challenges in AI-centric platforms, (ii) the need for diverse and up-to-date data foundations, (iii) LLM-based unified policy-code analysis, and (iv) dual usability for end-users and developers. We hope this SoK provides a shared foundation for future research on privacy policies and privacy documents.

</details>


### [162] [Tracing Provenance and Detecting Tampering with Complementary LLM Watermarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12713)
*Xiaoyan Feng,Yanjun Zhang,He Zhang,Leo Yu Zhang,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种新型水印方法，同时在LLM生成文本中嵌入鲁棒信号和脆弱信号，实现来源追踪与篡改检测的双重功能，支持Intact、Tampered和No-Watermark三种检测状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM水印虽能在编辑下保留来源归属，但这种鲁棒性同时也允许攻击者篡改关键内容却仍保留归属，形成"piggyback spoofing"（搭便车欺骗）漏洞。需要一个既能证明来源又能提供篡改证据的水印方案。

Method: 将鲁棒信号和脆弱信号共同嵌入每个生成的token中。两种信号使用相同的机制但采用独立密钥和不同的归一化文本种子窗口——一个对编辑有鲁棒性，另一个对读者可见的变更敏感。通过多轮无偏锦标赛重加权保持期望生成分布，周期性轮次分配模式控制两种信号间的权衡。检测时，两种信号分数构成二维空间，支持三种决策。

Result: 在两个大型语言模型和两个提示数据集上，该方法在篡改检测率上优于所有评估方法，同时保持竞争性的归属鲁棒性和困惑度。消融研究表明可靠的三态检测需要明确定义的完好性概念、双信号的共嵌入以及对编辑的互补敏感性。

Conclusion: 该水印方法通过共嵌入鲁棒与脆弱双信号，有效地解决了现有水印在篡改检测方面的不足，在不牺牲归属鲁棒性和文本质量的前提下实现了三态检测能力。

Abstract: Watermarking LLM-generated text is an important task for tracing its provenance. Existing LLM watermarks preserve provenance under editing, but this same robustness allows an adversary to alter critical content while retaining attribution, a vulnerability known as piggyback spoofing. We introduce an innovative watermark that jointly provides provenance and tamper evidence. It co-embeds a robust signal and a fragile signal into each generated token. The signals share the same mechanism but use independent keys and different seeding windows over normalized text, making one resilient to edits and the other sensitive to reader-visible changes. Multiple rounds of unbiased tournament reweighting preserve the expected generation distribution, while a periodic round-allocation pattern controls the trade-off between the two signals. At detection, their scores form a two-dimensional space supporting three decisions: Intact, Tampered, and No-Watermark. Across two large language models and two prompt datasets, our method demonstrates the highest tamper-detection rate among the evaluated methods while maintaining competitive attribution robustness and perplexity. Ablation studies show that reliable three-state detection requires a well-defined notion of intactness, co-embedding of the two signals, and complementary sensitivity to edits.

</details>


### [163] [PIPES: Securing Agent Perception with Provenance and Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12789)
*Sanjay Kariyappa,Severin Klingler,G. Edward Suh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PIPES（基于来源信息与先验约束的筛查）框架，用于防御工具使用型智能体面临的状态破坏攻击。该攻击利用工具响应的来源信息缺失，使受控内容夸大其信息权威并污染智能体对环境的认知。PIPES使用静态字段契约和语义先验对响应单元进行筛选，在VitaBench和AgentDyn数据集上将攻击成功率从84.7%降至2.3%，同时保持95%以上的良性工具效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用型智能体从不同可信度的外部源获取数据，但工具响应通常不标明每个组件的来源或其应传达的信息，这为攻击者创造了可乘之机：攻击者可在响应中注入受控内容，使其超越该响应组件的信息权威去声称环境状态，从而破坏智能体的环境感知，并使后续行为在现有防护机制看来是合理的。

Method: PIPES采用双轨筛查机制：(1) 对于具有稳定模式的响应，使用静态字段契约提供可靠的期望；(2) 对于开放文本内容，则结合响应前轨迹和受信任的来源元数据来条件化筛查。系统根据语义先验和来源层级对响应单元进行标记，部署方可选择移除、警告、屏蔽或升级处理检测到的违规。

Result: 在VitaBench的三个划分和AgentDyn的三个划分上，以Gemma 4 31B IT为目标智能体，PIPES将平均攻击成功率从84.7%降至2.3%，同时保持良性工具效用（有PIPES为92.5%，无防御为90.6%）。验证中还使用了自适应PAIR风格攻击。

Conclusion: PIPES通过结合来源信息（provenance）和语义先验对工具响应进行系统性筛查，能有效阻断状态破坏攻击，且不影响良性工具的实用性，为工具使用型智能体的安全防护提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Tool-using agents consume external data from sources with different levels of trust, yet tool responses rarely identify who produced each component or what it should convey. We show that this gap enables state-corruption attacks, in which attacker-controlled content makes environmental claims beyond the informational authority of its response component and corrupts the agent's perceived environment, making the resulting action appear justified to existing guardrails. We introduce PIPES (Provenance-Informed, Prior-Enforced Screening), which screens response units using semantic priors and source provenance. PIPES uses static field contracts when schemas provide stable expectations, and conditions screening of open-ended content on the pre-response trajectory and trusted provenance metadata. It marks units that violate their semantic prior or the provenance hierarchy; deployments may remove, warn, block, or escalate detected violations. We instantiate atomic removal and evaluate PIPES against adaptive PAIR-style attacks. Across the three VitaBench and three AgentDyn splits with Gemma 4 31B IT as the target agent, PIPES reduces average attack success from 84.7% to 2.3%, while preserving average benign utility (92.5% with PIPES versus 90.6% without defense).

</details>


### [164] [RealmEye: Virtual Machine Introspection for Arm CCA Realm VMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12822)
*Ruofei Qu,Wei Feng,Hongzhan Ma,Menghan Jia,Muyan Shen,Yu Qin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: RealmEye是首个针对Arm CCA Realm VM的虚拟机自省(VMI)系统。区别于依赖in-VM agent的现有方案(如00SEVen),RealmEye将全部自省逻辑置于Realm Management Monitor(RMM)的R-EL2层,实现了硬件强制的监控与受监控VM的隔离。它无需在Realm内部驻留agent,通过读取内存、寄存器,挂起VM获取一致性快照,以及页面级访问捕获,并利用周期性自驱动触发模式防止Hypervisor与rootkit串通。结果通过硬件证明通道返回远端所有者,兼容LibVMI和DRAKVUF等现有工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Confidential VMs(CVMs)的硬件隔离在保护VM免受恶意云攻击的同时,也导致所有者无法检测VM内部运行的恶意软件(如内核rootkit)。现有的CVM-VMI方案(00SEVen)依赖AMD SEV-SNP的VMPL0特权层,该机制在Arm CCA上不存在,导致Realm VM完全没有自省解决方案。因此需要一种在Arm CCA上无需修改Realm即可实现外部VM自省的机制。

Method: RealmEye将全部自省逻辑部署在RMM的R-EL2层(Realm Management Monitor的内部),实现了监控器与被监控Realm的硬件隔离。核心功能包括:读取Realm内存和寄存器、挂起VM以获取一致快照、页面级访问捕获,且不依赖任何in-VM接口。配备周期性自驱动触发模式,使扫描时序保持在RMM内部,阻止Hypervisor与in-Realm rootkit共谋。结果通过硬件证明通道返回远端所有者,并使用CCA驱动后端实现与LibVMI和DRAKVUF的互操作。

Result: 在Arm FVP(固定虚拟平台)上,RealmEye成功检测了Diamorphine rootkit的进程隐藏和syscall-table挂钩攻击。其in-RMM开销可通过原始调用计数线性预测,表明性能开销具有可预测性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: RealmEye填补了Arm CCA Realm VM缺乏VM自省解决方案的空白,通过将自省逻辑置于RMM实现了不修改Realm的、硬件强制的VM自省,为Confidential VM场景提供了新的安全监控范式,并兼容现有VMI工具生态。

Abstract: Confidential VMs (CVMs) have become the dominant substrate for sensitive cloud workloads, from financial services to privacy-preserving AI inference. The hardware isolation that protects these CVMs from a malicious cloud also blinds their owners to what runs inside them: kernel rootkits planted via network or supply-chain attacks can hide processes, tamper with kernel data, and exfiltrate model weights under the cover of the same isolation that defends the VM. Tenants therefore need to inspect a running CVM from outside, yet classical VM introspection (VMI) presupposes a trusted Hypervisor, which CVMs exclude from the TCB. The state-of-the-art CVM-VMI system, 00SEVen, restores introspection on AMD SEV-SNP via an in-VM agent at a privileged tier (VMPL0), a mechanism that does not exist on Arm CCA, leaving Realm VMs without any introspection solution.
  We present RealmEye, the first VMI system for Arm CCA Realm VMs. RealmEye places the entire introspection logic inside the Realm Management Monitor (RMM) at R-EL2, achieving hardware-enforced separation between the monitor and the monitored VM: no agent runs inside the Realm, and the Realm remains unmodified. RealmEye reads Realm memory and registers, suspends the VM for consistent snapshots, and traps page-level accesses, without relying on any in-VM interface. A periodic, self-driven trigger mode keeps scan timing internal to the RMM, preventing the Hypervisor from colluding with in-Realm rootkits. Results are returned to the remote owner over a hardware-attested channel, and a CCA driver backend lets existing tools such as LibVMI and DRAKVUF interoperate with RealmEye unchanged. On the Arm FVP, RealmEye detects process hiding and syscall-table hooking by Diamorphine, and its in-RMM cost is linearly predictable from primitive invocation counts.

</details>


### [165] [Beyond Source: An Empirical Study of Python Bytecode Security Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12853)
*Baihong Chen,Tian Xie,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对Python字节码作为安全工件进行了系统性的实证研究，分析了PyPI生态中103万多个发布包，发现7,388个包含字节码的工件（含228,578个.pyc文件），并评估了反编译器、CPython运行时健壮性及源码级可复现性，揭示了字节码与实际源码行为之间的"检查-执行鸿沟"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python包安全分析主要聚焦于源码层面，但Python运行时可以直接执行.pyc字节码文件、编译只模块和marshalled代码对象，导致安全审查人员看到的内容（源码）与实际执行的内容（字节码）之间可能存在差异。这种"检查-执行鸿沟"（inspection-execution gap）使得仅基于源码的静态安全分析存在盲区，因此有必要系统性地研究Python字节码作为安全工件的影响。

Method: 作者在PyPI生态中收集了1,034,843个发布工件，从中识别出7,388个含字节码的工件（包括228,578个.pyc文件，其中28,193个为工件内无对应源码的只编译文件）。研究采用四条路径：(1) 测量字节码在PyPI中的暴露程度；(2) 使用支持多版本（CPython 3.8-3.14）的反编译工具评估字节码的可分析性；(3) 通过对抗性字节码测试CPython运行时的健壮性；(4) 检验字节码发现能否从普通Python源码复现。此外还使用模糊测试生成变异字节码，检测崩溃与内存损坏特征。

Result: 对现代CPython 3.8-3.14字节码，所选反编译器对204,904个范围内文件中至少204,901个能输出源码（但仅衡量了输出行为而非功能等价性）。工具健壮性差：PyPI实际字节码触发托管代码异常和超时，对抗性变异字节码还会导致反编译器原生进程崩溃，共产生17种不同的健壮性签名。模糊测试产生1,009个堆栈去重后的运行时发现，主要症状为指针解引用；其中261个组表现出潜在内存损坏特征，至少91.7%的组在文档声明的"不安全输入边界"之外仍继续执行。所有这些发现均无法从普通Python源码复现。

Conclusion: Python字节码是生态中可见的安全工件、实际可分析的目标，且作为解释器输入其行为无需与源码级行为一致。这一发现意味着仅依赖源码的安全审查机制存在系统性盲区，需要将字节码纳入包安全审查和运行时防护的考量范围。

Abstract: Python package security is largely source-centric, yet Python runtimes can execute bytecode directly through .pyc files, compiled-only modules, and marshalled code objects, creating an inspection-execution gap. We present an empirical study of Python bytecode as a security artifact. We measure bytecode exposure in PyPI distributions, evaluate practical analyzability using version-aware tooling, assess CPython runtime robustness under adversarial bytecode, and test source-level reproduction of bytecode findings. Across 1,034,843 collected PyPI artifacts, we identify 7,388 bytecode-containing artifacts, including 228,578 .pyc files and 28,193 artifact-local source-less .pyc files. For modern CPython 3.8-3.14 bytecode, at least one selected decompiler emits source for 204,901 of 204,904 in-scope files, a result measuring emission rather than verified functional equivalence. Tools are non-robust: observed PyPI bytecode triggers managed-code exceptions and timeouts, while adversarial mutated bytecode also drives decompilers into native process failures; together these outcomes yield 17 distinct robustness signatures. Fuzzing produces 1,009 stack-deduplicated runtime findings dominated by pointer-dereference symptoms; 261 groups exhibit potential memory-corruption characteristics, and at least 91.7% of groups reach execution beyond the documented-unsafe ingestion boundary. None reproduce from ordinary Python source. Bytecode is thus a visible ecosystem artifact, a practical analysis target, and a security-relevant interpreter input whose behavior need not match source-level behavior.

</details>


### [166] [Discovering Persistent Behavioural Patterns for Interpretable Blockchain Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12864)
*Dorottya Zelenyanszki,Zhe Hou,Kamanashis Biswas,Vallipuram Muthukkumarasamy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可扩展、应用无关的框架，用于从大规模区块链活动中发现持久行为模式。该框架通过构建行为句子并结合两级嵌入（句子级与序列级嵌入），配合可解释的行为画像工具，在以太坊3000万+交易上成功识别出包括DEX交易、NFT活动、钓鱼、机器人操作、预言机操纵和拉地毯骗局等多种行为模式，并验证了模式在独立观察窗口间的稳定性，支持长期行为分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大规模DeFi分析大多针对特定应用场景设计，难以扩展，且结果难以解释。缺乏一个既能规模化处理区块链数据，又能提供可解释性，并支持跨时间窗口持久行为发现的通用框架。

Method: 框架首先构建包含合约、代币和市场上下文信息的行为句子，然后应用两级嵌入：句子级嵌入捕获单个行为动作，序列级嵌入捕获用户随时间的行为演化。最后通过一个可解释的行为画像工具，从行为模式、日常规律、时间动态、实体暴露度和可疑性证据等多个维度刻画发现的行为社区。

Result: 在以太坊上使用超过3000万笔交易进行评估，框架成功发现了常规与恶意两类行为模式，涵盖DEX交易、NFT活动、钓鱼攻击、机器人操作、预言机操纵和拉地毯骗局等。关键发现是许多模式在独立观察窗口间保持稳定，证明了框架能够识别超越单一分析周期的长期行为特征。

Conclusion: 该框架在可扩展性、可解释性和持久性分析三方面实现了结合，为区块链取证调查、行为归因和威胁发现提供了实用支持，填补了大规模区块链行为分析中通用性、可解释性与长期稳定性兼顾的空白。

Abstract: Public blockchain data enables large-scale DeFi-related analysis, but many existing approaches are application-specific, difficult to scale, or hard to interpret. This research proposes a scalable, application-agnostic framework for \emph{persistent behavioural pattern discovery} from large-scale blockchain activity. It constructs behaviour sentences enriched with contract, token and market context, then applies a two-step embedding process: sentence-level embeddings capture individual actions, while sequence-level embeddings capture user behaviour over time. An interpretable behavioural profiler characterizes discovered communities through behavioural motifs, routines, temporal dynamics, entity exposure, and suspiciousness evidence. Evaluation on Ethereum using over 30 million transactions shows that the framework uncovers both routine and malicious behavioural patterns, including decentralised exchange (DEX) trading, NFT activity, phishing, bot operations, oracle manipulation, and rug-pull schemes. Importantly, many patterns remain stable across independent observation windows, enabling the identification of long-term behaviours beyond a single analysis period. The proposed framework combines scalability, interpretability, and persistence analysis, supporting blockchain forensic investigation, behavioural attribution, and threat discovery.

</details>


### [167] [Labels Are Not Endpoints: Treatment Leakage and Construct Validity in MCP Agent Security Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12880)
*Rana Muhammad Ahmed,Sabahat Abbas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对一次保留的工具使用智能体安全评估活动进行测量审计，发现历史评分器存在处理标签泄漏问题（treatment元数据直接门控ATTACK_SUCCESS类别），通过盲重建修正58条历史标签，锁定v2普查中ATTACK_SUCCESS记录为零，并提出七链接完整性链与可执行的范围受限端点完整性检查器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全评估中常将存储标签等同于行为事实，这可能因标签泄漏导致测量偏差。作者希望通过追溯审计历史评估活动，验证标签与真实行为的对应关系，暴露潜在的处理泄漏问题。

Method: 作者追溯了10,200条执行记录，映射到180个模型绑定请求、45个语义请求和15个可观察刺激。对历史评分器进行泄漏审计，构建处理盲重建，实施锁定v2普查，并进行双审阅者盲一致性审查（共96个请求），最终提出七链接完整性链及端点完整性检查器。

Result: 发现历史评分器存在直接处理泄漏：treatment元数据门控ATTACK_SUCCESS类别。盲重建修正58条历史标签为授权良性完成，同时保留3个验证的保护数据转移和1个独立未授权转发案例。锁定v2普查中ATTACK_SUCCESS为0，转发案例在目标完成语义边界上仍为HIJACK_ATTEMPT。双审阅者一致通过96个请求的解释，但在4个构念边界案例上与锁定代码手册存在分歧。

Conclusion: 本研究贡献是一次活动界定的测量审计，而非群体攻击率、模型排名、防御效能或因果估计。结果表明历史评估标签不可直接作为行为事实，需要完整性链与盲审机制来保障测量可信度。

Abstract: Security evaluations of tool-using agents often equate stored labels with behavioral facts. We audit a preserved campaign by tracing 10,200 execution rows to 180 model-bound requests, 45 semantic requests, and 15 observable stimuli. Two schema treatments were delivered, but the planned external payload-family corpus was not. The historical grader exhibited direct treatment leakage: treatment metadata gated the ATTACK_SUCCESS class, so fixed behavior could change class under treatment relabeling. A treatment-blind reconstruction corrects 58 historical ATTACK_SUCCESS or HIJACK_ATTEMPT labels to authorized benign completions while preserving three verified protected-data transfers and one separate unauthorized-forwarding case. The locked v2 census contains exactly zero ATTACK_SUCCESS records, while the forwarding case remains a HIJACK_ATTEMPT at a semantic boundary concerning objective completion. A dual-reviewer blinded concordance review of all 96 requests deemed structurally interpretable by locked v2 produced identical reviewer-consensus classes but differed from the locked codebook on four construct-boundary cases. We contribute a seven-link Integrity Chain and an executable, scope-bounded endpoint-integrity linter. The result is a campaign-bounded measurement audit, not a population attack-rate, model-ranking, defense-efficacy, or causal estimate.

</details>


### [168] [Adversarial Robustness in Smishing Detection: A Comparative Analysis of Adversarial Fragility in Classical vs. Transformer-Based Detection Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12889)
*Denzel Chiuseni,Athanase Bahizire,Silva Hama,Jema David Ndibwile*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究评估了五种模型架构（三种经典词法模型和两种多语言Transformer）在短信钓鱼（smishing）检测中的对抗鲁棒性，使用27,037条消息数据集，通过三种攻击类型和多级强度测试，发现经典模型在字符混淆和结构扰动下近乎崩溃（RDR高达0.988），而Transformer更具韧性（RDR最高0.351），且干净文本性能不能预测对抗鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的smishing检测系统通常在干净、单语文本上训练和评估，但攻击者通过字符混淆、跨语言代码切换和结构扰动等方式规避系统，特别是在低资源场景下。此前缺乏对smishing检测模型在对抗攻击下的系统性鲁棒性评估。

Method: 使用27,037条消息数据集，对五种模型架构进行对抗鲁棒性评估：三种经典词法模型（Random Forest、XGBoost、CNN+BiLSTM）和两种多语言Transformer（mBERT、XLM-RoBERTa）。经典模型接受黑盒通用攻击，Transformer使用注意力引导的目标攻击。每种模型在三种攻击类型和多个强度级别下测试，用鲁棒性退化比（RDR）衡量性能，并使用Cliff's d效应量、Mann-Whitney U和Friedman检验进行统计验证。

Result: 经典模型在字符混淆和结构扰动下表现出近灾难性失败（RDR最高0.988），而Transformer表现出显著更高的韧性（RDR最高0.351），结构扰动是Transformer最明显的脆弱点。XLM-RoBERTa尽管干净文本基线更高，但退化程度大于mBERT。Cliff's d表明两类模型之间存在实质性差异，统计检验确认这些模式源于模型架构而非采样。

Conclusion: 干净文本性能不能作为对抗鲁棒性的可靠预测指标。不同架构在对抗攻击下表现出明确的分界线，需要架构特定化的防御策略。Smishing检测应被视为对抗性网络安全挑战而非静态分类任务。

Abstract: Smishing detection systems are commonly trained and evaluated on clean, monolingual text. In low-resource settings, however, attackers frequently circumvent these systems through character obfuscation, cross-lingual code-switching, and structural perturbation. This study evaluates adversarial robustness for five model architectures: three classical lexical models (Random Forest, XGBoost, CNN+BiLSTM) and two multilingual transformers (mBERT, XLM-RoBERTa), using a dataset of 27,037 messages. Classical models are subjected to black-box generic attacks, while transformers are evaluated with attention-guided targeting. Each model is tested across three attack types and intensity levels, with performance measured by the Robustness Degradation Ratio (RDR). The results reveal a distinct architectural boundary: classical models experience near-catastrophic failure under character obfuscation and structural perturbation (RDR up to 0.988), whereas transformers demonstrate significantly greater resilience (RDR up to 0.351), with structural perturbation representing their most pronounced vulnerability. Effect-size analysis (Cliff's d) indicates a substantial difference between the two model categories. Within the transformer group, XLM-RoBERTa, despite achieving a higher clean-text baseline, exhibits greater degradation than mBERT. These findings demonstrate that clean-text performance is not a reliable predictor of adversarial robustness. Statistical validation using Mann-Whitney U and Friedman tests confirms that these patterns are attributable to model architecture rather than sampling. The results underscore the necessity for architecture-specific defences and frame smishing detection as an adversarial cybersecurity challenge rather than a static classification task.

</details>


### [169] [Beyond Handcrafted Security: Towards Self-Evolving Defense for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12977)
*Jiajun Ruan,Peiyang Li,Yukun Chen,Fengting Li,Chao Feng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出HARD（基于命名的自主运行时防御演化）框架，将LLM代理的运行时防御从手工设计转变为自主演化过程，通过命名层级的公式化描述统一现有防御干预，并基于失败轨迹自动迭代改进防御机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理的扩展能力引入了复杂的安全威胁，现有运行时防御高度依赖人工设计干预，缺乏构建和维护这些防御机制的原则性框架。

Method: 首先建立命名层级的运行时防御公式化描述，系统刻画命名机制如何支持防御构建；在此基础上提出HARD框架，自动识别合适的干预策略并根据观察到的失败轨迹迭代改进防御制品。

Result: 大量实验表明HARD相较现有手工防御在安全性能上有所提升，同时保持良性任务效用不受影响。

Conclusion: 自主防御演化是保障已部署LLM代理安全的新范式，使代理能够识别自身防御弱点并持续改进保护机制。

Abstract: The expanding operational capabilities of large language model (LLM) agents introduce sophisticated security threats. Runtime defenses have emerged as an effective approach to mitigating these risks by integrating security mechanisms into the agent execution loop. However, existing runtime defenses rely heavily on manually designed interventions and lack a principled framework for their construction and maintenance. In this work, we first develop a harness-level formulation of runtime defense that systematically characterizes how harness mechanisms enable defense construction and provides a unified view of existing runtime defense interventions from a harness perspective. Building on this formulation, we propose HARD (Harness-based Autonomous Runtime Defense Evolution), a self-evolving runtime defense framework that automatically identifies appropriate intervention strategies and iteratively improves defense artifacts based on observed failure traces. HARD transforms runtime defense development from manual engineering into an autonomous evolution process, and extensive experiments demonstrate that it improves security performance over existing handcrafted defenses while preserving benign task utility. Our findings highlight autonomous defense evolution as a promising new paradigm for securing deployed LLM agents, enabling agents to identify defense weaknesses and continuously improve their protection mechanisms.

</details>


### [170] [ATOBench: Tracing How Autonomous Penetration-Testing Agents Verify Vulnerabilities When Target Evidence Lies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12996)
*Qiyang Chen,Yixi Li,Fengwei Zhang,Junlin Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ATOBench，一个通过注入响应变换使自主渗透测试智能体的验证过程可观察的评估框架，在450个episode上评估5种模型路线，揭示了增强的活动可能掩盖断裂的验证链，而成功的恢复取决于找到可用证据并在报告中保留它。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主渗透测试智能体依赖目标响应来引导后续行动和最终报告，但错误的响应会同时误导攻击轨迹和验证过程。然而，最终报告几乎不揭示智能体如何解释冲突证据、改变路线、决定停止或将观察转化为漏洞声明。缺乏对验证过程的可观察性，使得评估只能看到最终结果而无法理解过程中的推理和决策。

Method: ATOBench框架在运行时注入已注册的响应变换，将每个变换后的episode与相同环境下的原生episode配对，并在首个受影响的响应处对齐。通过源链接重建后续行动、证据恢复、停止和报告支持。定义了三个冻结的观察契约：漏洞利用证明、资源所有权和可复用工件。在450个episode上评估五种模型路线。

Result: 分析表明，增加的活动量可能掩盖断裂的验证链，而成功的恢复依赖于找到可用证据并将其保留到报告中。ATOBench将欺骗性目标观察转化为可复现的证据处理探针。

Conclusion: ATOBench提供了超越最终结果的进程级视图，通过揭示不可信观察如何影响行动、验证和报告，扩展了进攻性渗透测试智能体的评估范式。

Abstract: Autonomous penetration-testing agents rely on target responses. These responses guide both subsequent actions and the final report. A deceptive response can therefore redirect both the attack trajectory and the agent's verification process.
